4Q26/2027 年獲利將續創新高，受惠 DDR4 供不應求延續 
預估 4Q26 營收季增 13.2%至 1,512.46 億 元 ， 其 中 預 期ASP 再 季 增
15~20%、雖收斂但仍維持雙位數季增；毛利率季增 0.4ppts 至 86.1%，報
價維持高檔下與 3Q26 相近；EPS 季增 11.9%至 28.42 元。展望後續，月產
能未來四年內將由目前約 65kwpm 分階段新增 35.9kwpm 的產能、2H27 開
始貢獻，供需緊俏延續下 ASP 將維持高檔，4Q26/2027 年營運續創新高。 
2027 年 DRAM 供需緊俏與報價上行持續，主要由 Server 驅動 
本中心預期 2027 年 DRAM 持續供不應求，報價仍在上行循環，最主要驅動
力 Server 於整體位元需求佔比過半且供需差達 20%以上(高於整體 DRAM
的約 10%)，其中預期 2027 年 HBM 市場規模年增 243%，Nvidia VR200 搭
載 SOCAMM2 位元需求成長達 6 倍，General Server 亦佔整體 Server 需求
一半比重，相關 RDIMM、LPDRAM 成長強勁；同時新增產能相對需求成長
仍有限，三大原廠 2027 年底 DRAM 投片月產能僅年增 10~15%，中國同業
長鑫存儲雖擴產積極仍僅佔整體產能約 15%，主要供應中國國內需求。此外，
DDR4 於消費性/網通/Server 仍具龐大需求，目前 AI Server 仍有約 10%週
邊應用搭載 DDR4，供給則在國際原廠 EOL 下大幅縮減，長鑫存儲重心亦
已轉往 DDR5，預估 2027 年 DDR4 仍有 20%以上的供需缺口(詳見圖 7)。 
產品組合持續往 Server/AI 提升，中長期營運展望正向 
綜 合 上 述 ， 預 估2026/27 年 營 收 年 增 525.5%/66.8% 至 4,165.31 億元
/6,946.17 億元 ，毛利率年增 60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5% ，EPS 年增
3457.6%/68.6%至 75.93/128.01 元。考量 1) 公司現為 DDR4 第一大廠且同
時具備 DDR5 產品布局、2) 產品組合持續往 Server/AI 提升、3) WoW 客製
化產品有望帶來更穩定獲利支撐，中長期營運更加穩健，以 2.5 倍 PB(過去
三年落在 0.4~2.8 倍)、2027 年 BVPS 2 30.7 元，給予買進、目標價 570 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
4Q26 預期 ASP 再季增 15~20%，2027 年 DDR4 供不應求延續下合約
價仍維持高檔，獲利將年增 68.6%續創新高。 
⧫  
2027 年 DRAM 產業 供 需 緊 俏 及 報 價 上 行 持 續 ， 最 主 要 驅 動 力 為
Server 相關 DRAM 訂單需求大幅成長，新增產能則相對有限。 
⧫  
產品組合續往 Server/AI 提升，中長期營運更加穩健，給予買進、目標
價 570 元(2.5x 2027 年 BVPS)。

營運分析
3Q26 獲利季增 74.6%，主因 ASP 季增>60%
3Q26 營收 1,336.49 億元，季增 61.9%/年增 611.7%，其中 1) 平均售價(ASP)：季增超過 60%/年
增超過 500%， 受 惠 半 年 期/一年期等 LTA 重 新 議 價 及 季 度 合 約 價 調 整 、2) 位 元 出 貨 量(Bit
shipment)：季減中個位數百分比 /年減低個位數百分比，主因新廠產能尚未開出且庫存處於低水位；
毛利率達 85.7%，季增 6.2ppts/年增 67.2ppts，受惠 ASP 大幅成長；營業利益率 80%，季增
6.3ppts/年增 74ppts，費用方面提列當期及未來之員工薪酬增加；稅後獲利 876.27 億元，季增
74.6%/年增 5505.1%；EPS 25.40 元，整體 3Q26 獲利大致符合市場預期。

4Q26 DDR4 供需持續緊俏，ASP 預估仍延續雙位數季增幅但收斂至 15~20%
展望 4Q26，預估營收季增 13.2%/年增 402.6%至 1,512.46 億元，其中  1) ASP ：預估季增
15~20%，考量 DDR4 供需緊俏延續，雖季增幅收斂且本季較無如 3Q26 LTA 重新議價的挹注，但
仍預估季增雙位數百分比、2) 位元出貨量：考量 5A 新廠產能尚待 2H27 起陸續開出，此前僅能以
生產效率提升與 16nm 製程微幅增加貢獻，且庫存仍處低水位，預估季 減低個位數百分比；毛利率
季增 0.4ppts/年增 37ppts 至 86.1%，預期在報價維持高檔下毛利率與 3Q26 水準相近；營業利益
率季增 0.4ppts/年增 41.2ppts 至 80.4%；稅後獲利季增 11.9%/年增 784%至 980.62 億元；EPS

28.42 元續創新高。

供不應求延續下，2027 年 DRAM 合約價仍維持高檔且獲利續創新高
本次法說會重點包括：1) 公司表示 DDR4 市場環境健康，DDR4 目前佔整體 DRAM 市場需求仍有
約 12~14%，雖需求逐漸縮小但對應大幅縮減的供給仍相當強勁，ASP 仍有望保持雙位數成長；2)
2026 年資本支出指引不超過新台幣 697 億元，2026~2029 年 5A 新廠資本支出 預算約新台幣
3,466 億元，屏東新廠長期資本支出規劃達 100 億美元，主要用於 AI 應用的 WoW 3D 晶圓封裝與

測試需求，預計 2H28 起設備裝機； 3) Server/AI 相關目前營收占比 >20%，公司指引將成長至
2029~2030 年的 40~50%，預期隨新產能上線持續提升。
展望 2026/2027 年，目前月產能推估約 65kwpm，而 5A 新廠具備約 50kwpm 的擴充空間，目前規
劃 2028 年將投產至 30kwpm，新增產能預計於 2H27 開始微幅貢獻，相關資本支出預算約新台幣
3,466 億元(含 EUV 設備)，長期而言公司亦預計於屏東科學園區投資設立 3D 晶圓封裝測試廠，導
入矽穿孔(TSV)、晶圓堆疊等 3DIC 先進技術；位元出貨量方面，2026 年在晶圓產出尚待新廠產能
開出、以生產效率提升與 16nm 製程微幅增加貢獻下，公司維持年增 high-10s % 的指引，2027 年
考量高基期且新廠產能於下半年才開始貢獻，預估 2H27 前位元出貨量維持平穩。
ASP 則受惠市場供需緊俏，1~3Q26 每季皆季增約 60~70%，4Q26 估亦將維持 15~20%的雙位數

季增幅；進入 2027 年，DDR4 供不應求態勢延續，但考量報價已處高檔、非 AI 下游終端漸難以負
荷，且記憶體原廠毛利率多已達 80%以上，預估 2027 年各季 ASP 持平至季增個位數百分比。
綜合上述，預估 2026/2027 年營收年增 525.5%/66.8%至 4,165.31 億元/6,946.17 億元；毛利率年
增 60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5%，2026 年毛利率受惠 ASP 大幅年增已顯著提升至高水位，2027
年報價上行趨勢持續但逐漸持穩於高檔，折舊方面， 2026 年公司預估資本支出以不超過 新台幣
697 億元為上限，其中約 50%用於設備採購，2027 年隨 5A 新廠投產所需設備投資持續增加，資
本支出將大幅年增，故在 2026 年隨先前廠房設備折舊陸續到期使折舊成本相對低之後，2027 年折
舊金額將明顯增加，但在目前 ASP 與獲利皆維持高檔且加計新產出貢獻下，整體 2027 年毛利率受
折舊影響可控；營益率年增 68.8ppts/2.2ppts 至 76.7%/78.9%；稅後獲利年增 3861%/68.6%至

2,619.4 億元/4,416.29 億元；EPS 75.93/128.01 元。
除報價與產能拉升外，中長期可關注趨勢包括： 1) Server/AI 相關應用占比持續提升、 2) DDR5
16Gb 6400/7200Mbps 及以上貢獻增加、3) WoW 客製化高頻寬記憶體放量。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第5 頁，共 20頁  
產品結構分析 
受惠 DDR4 供需緊俏及寡佔優勢、Server/AI 占比續增，亦具備 DDR5 產品布局 
雖三大國際原廠 DDR4 EOL 以將產能轉向 HBM/DDR5，中國同業長鑫存儲(CXMT)亦將重心轉往
DDR5，但 DDR4 於消費性/網通/Server 等仍具龐大需求，在供給大幅縮減下，公司目前已為全球
第一大 DDR4 供應商，高度寡占且具備價格主導權，除 8Gb DDR4 以外，16Gb 亦於 2H26 開始貢
獻營收。公司目前 Server/AI 相關應用營收比重已超過 20%，且占比將隨新產能開出而提升，持續
受惠 Storage、BMC、NIC、Switch 應用等貢獻，相較國內同業有望減緩消費性疲弱影響。 
DDR4 為公司目前產業地位、獲利最有利的產品組合，但其亦具備 DDR5 技術能力與相關營收貢獻，
目前採 1B 製程的 16Gb DDR5 已量產出貨，傳輸速率現以 6400Mbps 為主並持續開發 7200Mbps
及以上規格。未來若終端客戶需求加速由 DDR4 轉向 DDR5，公司得以靈活調整產能配置以增加出
貨，持續拿下市場份額。除一般型 DRAM 以外，公司亦有 LPDDR4 產品出貨，LPDDR5 則有望於
2027 年導入量產。 
圖 8：目前營收仍以 DDR4 為主

圖 9：1B/1C 營收占比持續提升

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
WoW 客製化高頻寬記憶體為長期成長利基，布局 Edge AI 與外溢市場需求 
WoW(Wafer-on-Wafer)客製化高頻寬記憶體為有利公司中長期營運體質的重要成長動能。目前的標
準 HBM 為 IC 對 IC 堆疊，主要用於 Cloud AI 訓練等市場，然而 Edge 端裝置受限於功耗、成本與
物理體積等限制無法直接採用，同時三大國際原廠有限之 HBM 產能亦無法完全滿足整體 Cloud 端
市場需求，公司亦將受惠此外溢效果。其客製化產品採用 Wafer-on-Wafer Hybrid Bonding( 晶圓級
混合鍵合)技術，將 DRAM 與邏輯 Core Die 直接對接堆疊，能以顯著之成本與功耗優勢滿足客戶於
Edge 端的高頻寬需求。目前有多個客戶專案進行中，預估 2H27~2028 年可見較清晰的量產營收，
新廠產能規劃上亦保留彈性予 WoW 客製化 DRAM 專案，長線有望為公司提供較穩定的獲利支撐。 
圖 10：Wafer-on-wafer 堆疊技術示意圖

資料來源：Samsung、元大投顧整理

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第6 頁，共 20頁  
財務分析 
整體財務結構於產業上行及下行循環期間皆維持穩健 
公司在 2020~2022 年 上 行 循 環 期 間 ， 流 動 比 率 與 速 動 比 率 分 別 高 達2020/2021/2022 年的
874%/678%/652%及 707%/599%/502%，後續下行期間雖降至 2023/2024 年的 480%/329%及
339%/217%，但整體仍位於健康水準，且負債比率在上行及下行期間均維持在低於 20%的低水準，
短長期償債能力無虞。2025 年以來隨著記憶體市況轉佳、報價顯著上漲，加上 1H26 完成 787.18
億元之私募案，流動比率與速動比率再回升至 2Q26 的 691%與 635%，負債比率則為 17%，預期
後續持續優化。 
經營能力方面，應收/應付帳款週轉天數落在 36~60 天/19~48 天的健康區間，且兩者差距在合理範
圍內。然而，存貨週轉天數受記憶體景氣循環影響，由上行循環期間 2020/2021 年的 130/97 天，
攀升至下行循環期間 2023/2024 年的 271/333 天，使整體現金轉換週期由 2021 年的 120 天拉長至
2024 年 337 天，反映景氣谷底期間庫存積壓對營運資金運轉效率帶來短期壓力。不過隨著 2025
年以來 DRAM 報價顯著上漲且公司庫存去化加速，2Q26 存貨週轉天數已降至 124 天，帶動現金轉
換週期縮短至 118 天，預期將持續改善。 
觀察現金流與資本支出動向，公司於 2021 年達到 328.47 億元的自由現金流高峰，隨後於
2022~2024 年下行期間，公司雖降低但仍投入 100 多億元的資本支出以推動製程技術組合轉往
10nm 級發展，自由現金流 2023/2024 年轉為淨流出 193.4/141.91 億元。儘管自由現金流短暫承壓，
公司在手現金仍穩健維持於 500 億元以上之充沛水準， 2025 年進入上行循環後營運顯著轉佳，
1H26 自由現金流已達 805.52 億元，加上引進 Kioxia、SanDisk、Solidigm 及 Cisco 之高達 787.18
億元的私募案挹注，使 2Q26 底在手現金大幅提升至 2,176.03 億元的歷史新高，且隨著營運成長
將持續增加，足以支應 5A 新廠未來四年高達 3,466 億元之資本支出規劃，整體財務結構穩健。

產業概況  
2027 年 DRAM 供需緊俏與報價上行持續，主要由 Server 需求驅動 
本中心預期 2027 年 DRAM 將持續供不應求，報價仍在上行循環，其中最主要驅動力為 Server 相
關 DRAM 訂單需求大幅成長且優於預期，Server 佔整體 DRAM 位元需求比重持續增加，預期自
2026 年的 54%成長至 2027 年的 63%，供需差更達 20%以上，高於整體 DRAM 的約 10%，主因：
1) Server 需求大幅成長，如本中心預估 2026/2027 年 ASIC 晶片量年增 91%/88%；2) Server 單位
DRAM Content 持續提升；3) AI Inference/Agentic AI 帶動 DRAM 儲存需求多樣化，如擴大導入
LPDDR(SOCAMM)於 Server 架構中，其中預估 2027 年 Nvidia VR200 機櫃對應 CPU 搭配的
SOCAMM2 位元需求成長高達 6 倍。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第8 頁，共 20頁  
預期 2027 年 HBM 整體市場規模年增 243%，其中位元出貨量年增 55%、ASP 翻倍成長，主因：1) 
HBM 先前因年度議價機制無法反映 2H25 以來 DRAM 漲勢，近期已重新議定 2027 年度合約價；2) 
HBM4 放量出貨，其中 HBM4 佔整體 HBM 產值比重將達 46%。此外，雖 Rubin Ultra 、TPU v9 搭
配的 HBM4E 將由 12Hi 降至 8Hi，但主要為因應供給不足下為確保出貨顆數的應對措施，並非需求
下修。2027 年 HBM 市場份額預期 SK Hynix 約 40~45%、Samsung 約 35~40%、Micron 約
25~30%，SK Hynix 仍為 HBM 龍頭，但因 Samsung HBM4 進度優於同業、SK Hynix 持續調整
HBM4 效能，使 Samsung 市佔率具提升空間。 
圖 21：2027 年 HBM 市場規模將年增 243% 圖 22：HBM 佔整體 DRAM 投片比重持續提升

資料來源：元大投顧預估 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
以 BOM Cost 分布來看，DRAM/NAND 漲價對於 General Server 與 PC 價格之影響程度較高，如
DRAM/NAND 漲 10%，對於 AI Server/ General Server/PC/Smartphone 整機價格之影響分別為
1%/5.3%/3~3.6%/2~3%。 
而組裝廠更改記憶體規格之彈性來看，PC 最高、Server 其次、AI Server 接續、Smartphone 最低。
其中 PC 品牌廠因各地所需之規格不同且主機板可調整彈性相對手機高，加上每年都會針對原有的
零組件對供應商要求降價，因此可以最快調整其記憶體設計以因應記憶體價格變化，因此將較快出
現產品降規之情況；Server 部分以 Storage Server 所受之影響最大，尤其對於以中小企業客戶為
主之白牌業者其價格敏感度較高，不過對 CSP 來說對於 General Server/AI Server( 占比小)漲價之
接受度仍高；而 Smartphone 則只能在下一代產品發布前更改設計，多只能自行吸收或是對下游反
映售價，對於品牌廠毛利與終端需求影響較大。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第9 頁，共 20頁  
2027 年原廠 DRAM/NAND 新增產能有限，仍不足以因應強勁成長的需求 
雖 2027 年中部份新產能將上線，三大 DRAM 國際原廠年底投片月產能預估皆僅較 2026 年底增加
10~15%，長鑫存儲擴產幅度雖達 20~30%仍難以滿足中國國內需求 (目前中國 DRAM 自給率僅
30~40%)，即使搭配製程轉換以提升位元產出，仍 無法滿足本中心預估之全球 DRAM 位元需求量
30%以上的強勁成長，其中國際原廠擴產計畫如下： 
1) Samsung ：P4L 自 3Q25 起陸續投產，預計至 2027 年底月產能將達約 150kwpm，主要用於 1c 
DRAM 以因應強勁的 HBM4 需求，並持續推動現有廠房舊製程轉往 1c nm 以滿足 HBM 與通用型
1c DRAM 市場；此外，P5L Fab 1 已於 2025 年底復工、Fab 2 提前至 2026 年 7 月動工，產能目
標高於現有廠房約 50%以上。 
2) SK Hynix ：M15X 2026 下半年預計投產約 40kwpm，2027 年底月產能將達約 80kwpm，主要用
於 1b 並部分導入 1c DRAM ；公司亦表示 Y1 首個無塵室設備裝機將提前 3 個月至 2027 年 5 月，
以因應強勁的 AI 相關記憶體需求，預計至 2030 年上半年分階段開出共 360kwpm 的產能，Y2 則
預計 2027 年 7 月動工、2029 年 6 月投產，用於生產 HBM 及下世代 DRAM。 
3) Micron ：美國 ID1 預計 2027 年中產出第一批晶圓、ID2 則預計 2028 年底產出；向力積電添購
之銅鑼廠預計提前一季至 2027 年中出貨；新加坡廠區 HBM 封裝產能將於 2027 年上半年開始貢獻。 
4) 長鑫存儲：上海新廠 2027 年開始投產，主要針對 HBM3 等高端記憶體產品，亦新建後段封測廠
以填補中國在 TSV 及堆疊封裝的供應鏈空白，合肥 Phase 3 則預計以 LPDDR4X/5/5X 等產品為主，
預計 2028 年開始投產。

獲利預估與股票評價
正向看待南亞科後續營運，目標價 570 元
展望後續，在 ASP 維持高檔且新廠產能將於 2H27 開始逐步貢獻下，正向看待公司中長期營運成
長，預估 2026/2027 年營收年增 525.5%/66.8%至 4,165.31 億元/6,946.17 億元 ，毛利率年增
60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5%，EPS 年增 3457.6%/68.6%至 75.93/128.01 元。
目前公司股價約在 1.8 倍 2027 年每股淨值(BVPS) ( 隱含本益比 3.2 倍)，低於國外同業平均的 2.0
倍，公司過去三年 PB 則落在 0.4~2.8 倍區間。雖目前循環處於 DRAM 報價仍維持高檔但預期後續
季增幅將收斂的階段，然而考量 1) 公司為 DDR4 第一大廠的產業地位且同時具備 DDR5 產品布局、
2) 產品組合將逐步擺脫消費性並持續往 Server/AI 提升(目前相關營收占比已超過 20%)、3) WoW

客製化產品有望帶來更穩定的獲利支撐，有助公司中長期營運更加穩健，以 2.5 倍 PB、2027 年
BVPS 230.7 元，推得目標價 570 元，給予買進評等。

公司簡介  
南亞科技成立於 1995 年，近年於全球 DRAM 市佔率約 1~2%，排名第五，公司目前以 DDR4 為主(佔比 60~70%)，亦具備
DDR5 產品，以應用別而言，PC & Server 約 25~30%、Mobile 約 10~15%、Consumer 約 60~70%。公司生產基地為新北
泰山 3A 廠，月產能 6~6.5 萬片。由於 DDR4/5 為長期主流市場，公司將陸續發展 1B/1C/1D 製程，且預計 2026~2029 年
投入新台幣 3,466 億元建置 5A 新廠產能，預估新產能將於 2H27~2028 年開出。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 南亞科整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中等風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行在略為領先的位
置，但在半導體設計與製造的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 南亞科的整體曝險屬於中等水準，與半導體設計與製造行業的平均水準相當。公司較需要改善
的 ESG 議題包含商業道德以及人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：南亞科在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬較佳。ESG 報告體系完善，公
司已指定董事會成員負責監督 ESG 相關事宜。使用 ESG 目標評估高階主管績效；然而，這些目標並未在薪酬政策中明
確規定。儘管如此，公司的檢舉人保護計畫和環境政策均較為完善。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
2H26 Driven by Semis and
Photonics; 2027 Seen as
Another Strong Year
A unique combination of power, electrical and optical testing
tool capabilities position the company well in AI deployment.
Stay OW.
We see 3Q26 EPS of NT$13.71: Chroma's 3Q26 revenue came in at NT$17,926mn,
up 32% QoQ and 180% YoY. We believe the strength was driven mainly by the
semiconductor and photonics businesses, while the power testing business likely

remained at an elevated level. Within the semiconductor and photonics businesses,
SL T  should benefit from AI HPC customers' ramping; meanwhile, optical testing
tools for transceivers and, to a lesser extent, CPO, are also seen as growth drivers
during the quarter. We estimate GM of 60.5% (flat QoQ) as scale tailwinds should
be largely offset by a less favorable product mix, while OPM should expand by
0.5ppt QoQ to 39.3% on some operating leverage. Overall, we expect EPS of NT

$13.71 (+13% QoQ/+15% YoY).
Growth momentum seen sustained into 2027: Looking ahead, we expect Chroma
to see another year of strong growth, supported by the power testing (51% growth
for 42% mix) and semi & photonics businesses (58% growth for 40% mix).
• Power testing: The ever-increasing power consumption of AI clusters should
continue to drive demand for higher spec testing tools. Along with our
forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit demand in the next

three years (see report), we think this should stay a major driver for Chroma.
• SLT: We are seeing more HPC chip designers adopting the system level
testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. As the first mover in this market. Chroma should benefit from
increasing chip volume as well as higher TDP, in our view.
• Photonics: Transceivers should continue to be the mainstream solution;
architectures such as CPO and NPO also need testing tools.

• Emerging business: We expect Chroma, by leveraging its existing customer
relationship and technology expertise, to gain more traction in FT handlers
and burn-in ovens for AI HPCs in 2027. These are the markets that Chroma
previously focused less on, so they represent new opportunities.
Stay OW: We remain constructive on Chroma for its testing equipment's unique
combination of power, electrical and optical capabilities, allowing it to capture
growth opportunities in various aspects of AI deployment. Our PT remains NT

$2,800 as our estimate increases are offset by a lower target multiple of 40x 2027e
P/E (previously 45x) to factor in generally more conservative sentiment toward the
AI space now. This implies a PEG of 0.6 (vs. 1.0 over the past decade).

A Closer Look
Test instrument and ATS segment set to reach another
record high in 2026
Revenue for the segment increased 55% YoY to NT$10,546mn (37% of the mix) in 2025.
We estimate it will rise 118% YoY to NT$24,564mn (40% of the mix) in 2026. The ever-
increasing power consumption of AI clusters should continue to drive demand for higher
spec testing tools. Along with our forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit

demand in the coming three years (see our report), this should remain a major driver for
Chroma, in our view.

Multiple drivers in the semiconductor business
System-level testing  (SL T) business
SL T should benefit from the capex cycle of NVIDIA's Rubin generation GPU, AMD's MI450
GPU and more ASIC accelerators.  Management pointed out that discrete testing tools are
still preferred  by major customers.  We are also seeing more HPC chip designers adopting

the system level testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. We believe Chroma, as the first mover in this market, should benefit from
increasing chip volume, as well as higher TDP.
Metrology business
The metrology business should continue to benefit from current advanced packaging
architectures from its foundry and OSAT customers. It is confident that the performance
of its tools can win future architecture projects, which our industry checks suggest should

have higher metrology density.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Exhibit 3: Chroma –  Offerings for Metrology in Advanced Packaging
Source: Chroma
Chroma's metrology equipment employs white light interferometry technology. The basic
working principle is that white light is emitted on the reference mirror and the sample,
which are placed at the same distance from the light source. The reflected beams from
the reference mirror and sample surface are then received by a light sensor.
Interference occurs when two light beams collide, which could cause  them to either

strengthen or weaken, depending on the phase of the two light beams. Thus, by
measuring the intensity of the combined light beams, the distance between light sensor
and multiple points on the target surface can be calculated through an algorithm. This can
then be used to measure the dimensions of the structure or the alignment of layers.
Exhibit 4: Concept of Light Interference
Source: Keyence
Exhibit 5: Setup of Interferometer
Source: Wikipedia
CPO contribution to start from 2026

Chroma has been qualified for optical engine testing in Insertion 3 and system-level
testing in Insertion 4, and there might be incremental opportunities if supply chain players'
progress falls behind for testing equipment in Insertion 2. Over the longer term, Chroma
also  aspires to participate in the PIC optical/electrical/reliability testing in Insertion 1.

FT handlers to add another revenue stream
In HPC testing, Chroma used to focus mostly on the SL T side, although it did unveil its first
HPC FT handler earlier this year and is now going through the qualification process. We
believe this is a natural extension of its expertise and customer relationships accumulated
in SL Ts. We expect Chroma to gain more traction in FT handlers for AI HPCs in 2027.

Overall, we forecast semiconductor and photonics revenue will grow  129%  YoY in 2026,
accounting for 36% of Chroma's mix.

### Page 10

10
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Our price target (base case scenario value) remains  NT$2,800: Our PT is based on a
target multiple of 40x 2027e P/E (vs. 45x 2027e previously).   We lowered our multiple to
factor in recent volatility in market sentiment toward AI.   We view our multiple as justified
based on our estimated 68% operating profit CAGR over 2025-28. This implies a PEG of
0.6 vs. its average of 1.0 in the past decade, when the stock was trading at an average of

~19x forward P/E with an operating profit CAGR of 20%.
Bull case scenario value – NT$3,900 (unchanged): We apply a 55x multiple (vs. 62x P/E
previously)  to our earnings estimate for 2027.  This scenario reflects stronger  5G and HPC
demand, driving more earnings upside.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,100 (unchanged): We apply a 16x P/E multiple (vs. 18x
P/E previously) to our earnings estimate for 2027. This scenario factors in weaker HPC
revenue as hyperscalers start to focus more on ROI for AI capex than they did in the past

couple of years.
Risks:
• Softer-than-expected AI capex
• Slower progress in advanced packaging capacity expansion
• Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand

Risk Reward - Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
NT$2,800.00
Base case, 40x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it
better captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average
valuation of ~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating proﬁt CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG
of 1.0. We think our target multiple of 40x is justiﬁed by the 68% operating proﬁt CAGR we
project for 2025-28.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

UMC (2303.TW)

UMC
Company description
United Microelectronics Corporation (UMC) is a leading global semiconductor
foundry company. It provides high-quality IC fabrication services, focusing on
logic and various specialty technologies to serve all major sectors of the
electronics industry. UMC's comprehensive IC processing technologies and
manufacturing solutions include Logic/Mixed-Signal, embedded High-
Voltage, embedded Non-Volatile-Memory, RFSOI, and BCD. Most of UMC's

12-inch and 8-inch fabs with core R&D are located in Taiwan.
Investment strategy
We rate UMC shares a Buy. Overall inventory levels in PC, smartphone, and
consumer electronics are currently healthy, and we see limited inventory
correction downside. While macro uncertainties may linger, we believe UMC’s
niche technology offerings will help it outperform its trailing-edge foundry
peers. We also expect transfer orders from TSMC and the AI-on-device trend

to drive stronger demand, and see better UTR recovery for UMC going
forward.
Valuation
Our target price for UMC of NT$125 is based on 18x 2027 /28E EPS, which is
higher than the midpoint of its 3-year forward PE. We believe our target
multiple is justified by our belief that UMC should benefit from the robust
demand in 22/28nm, TSMC's increasing order outflow opportunity, long-term
opportunity in advanced packaging and photonics and that its niche

technology offerings should help it outperform its trailing-edge foundry
peers. Our target price equates to 2027E/28E PB of 3.8x/3.6x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) a longer-than-expected
demand recovery; 2) more competition from peers in 28nm; 3) worse-than-
expected developments from the company's China investment; 4) a
significant decline in smartphone end-demand; 5) poor contribution from
Novatek's iPhone OLED business; and 6) fiercer ASP competition from

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

營收走勢圖

研究員/游周翰 Jeffery.Yu@uni-psg.com

研究部重申華星光買進建議，主要考量：(1)管理層維持下半年營運優
於上半年展望，受惠 800G DCI 及 CW Laser 需求持續成長，4Q26 單月
營收有望挑戰 6 億元；(2)隨晶粒稼動率及良率持續改善，研究部上修
2027 年晶粒出貨量預估，加上 EML 等產品逐步放量，有望帶動產品組
合優化及獲利能力提升。綜上所述，研究部預估華星光 2026/2027 年
營收，由原先 60.6 億元/119.8 億元調整為 58.4 億元/120.8 億元
(+33%/107%YoY)，EPS 由原先 7.69 元/15.88 元調整為 7.5 元/16.35
元。考量2027 年產能擴充及相關產品出貨成長，營收與獲利有望呈現
倍數成長，維持目標價 800 元的買進建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26營收優於研究部預期，惟受匯率影響EPS僅符合預期： 
華星光於 10/14 公 佈 自 結 ，9 月營收 5.75 億元（ +5.3%MoM 、
+97.8%YoY） ，稅後淨利1.03億元，稅後淨利率達17.9%，EPS達
0.72元。相較於8月回升，除了匯率回穩外，亦受到營收規模放
大所影響 。 累計3Q26營收為16.29億元 （+26.4%QoQ，+51.4%YoY），
稅後淨利達3.26億元，EPS為2.28元，創歷史新高。若依研究部
過去費用率及業外假設回推，3Q26毛利率約28-29%（約+6ppts 
QoQ） ，主要受惠先前財稅認列時間差回補、產能利用率提升及
產品組合優化 。 整體而言 ，3Q26營收優於研究部預期16.19億元 ，
EPS僅符合預期2.26元 ， 推測主因受到匯率所影響毛利率及業外
損益。

表一、3Q26 研究部預估與實際差異 
單位：百萬元 實際 研究部原估 差異 
營收 1,630 1,613 +1% 
稅前淨利 394 417 -5.5% 
稅後淨利 326 333 -2.2% 
EPS(元) 2.28 2.34 -2.6% 
資料來源： 華星光自結、統一研究部預估

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 下半年營運優於上半年，惟毛利率受新產品良率等因素影響 ： 
展望4Q26， 管理層維持2H26營運優於1H26， 除了既有DCI產品持
續成長外，CW Laser需求持續提升、800G DCI進入批量生產，
以及EML與高增益APD開始貢獻營收，有望帶動整體營運向上，
單月營收有望挑戰6億元 。 近期經研究部通路訪查 ，800GZR出貨
量略低於原先預估，因此研究部下修 4Q26出貨量從75K下修至
55K，4Q26營收從原先預估19.6億元下修至17.31億元。此外考
量800GZR毛利率因良率及協助客戶購買稀釋毛利率不如原先研
究部預期的情況下，將EPS從2.51元下修至2.29元。

3. 隨產能擴充，營收獲利將持續成長： 
展望未來，在2027年產能倍增情況下，營收規模有望呈現倍數
成長。晶粒方面，除了既有的CW Laser產能擴充外，亦增加EML
晶粒產能，使公司在InP產品線有多種產品分散風險。此外，近
期經供應鏈訪查，晶粒稼動率及良率提升下，有望使整體晶粒
出貨量優於原先預期。因此研究部預估2027年整體晶粒出貨量
從4700萬顆上修至5000萬顆。綜上所述，研究部預估營收2027
年營收從119.8億元上修至120.8億元 ， EPS從15.88元微幅上修
至16.35元。

表二、研究部預估調整 
資料來源： 華星光自結、統一研究部預估

本次報告：電話訪談

矽力*-KY (6415 TT) 
資料中心與車載為成長主軸

永豐觀點 
矽力杰 3Q26 營收優於預期，4Q26 展望正向，雖短期毛利率較為波動，不過隨資
料中心與車載訂單加持，以及第四代製程平滲透率提升， 應有助於中長期毛利率回
穩。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：研究部預估矽力杰 2026 年稅後 EPS 11.95 元(原預估 11.59 元)，2027
年稅後 EPS 15.34 元。考量矽力杰資料中心與車載仍將持續帶動營運拓展，研究部
維持買進建議，目標價維持 644 元(42X 2027 年 EPS 評價)。

ESG 評析 
矽力*-KY 企業永續評鑑整體為 B 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面
向平均得分分別為 B+等與 B 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2023 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

矽力杰為電源管理晶片大廠：矽力杰成立於 02/2008，為電源管理 IC 設計公司，
產品線包括 DC/DC 轉換晶片、電池充電管理晶片、過流保護晶片、靜電防護裝置
與 LED 背光驅動晶片、能源監控管理晶片等。矽力杰 2Q26 產品終端應用主要區
分為四大領域，包括消費電子(如平板電腦、STB 與 TV)36%、工業應用(如 LED 照
明、伺服器與安防設備等)35%、資訊產品(如筆電)9%與網路通訊(如路由器、手機
等)6%、 車載14%。國際電源管理晶片設計同業與矽力杰產品類似者為 TI、Maxim、
ON Semi 與 ADI，台系類似的參與廠商則包括致新、茂達等。 
3Q26 營收優於預期 ，4Q26 展望正向 ：矽力杰總計 3Q26 營收 72.83 億元
(+21%QoQ，+53%YoY)，落於研究部原預估 3Q26 營收達成率 102%(研究部原
預估季增 18%)，優於預期，主要係由資料中心為最主要推升力道，再者車載因新
款晶片推出與市占率提升而增 、 消費電子即便市場需求較為疲弱， 但因公司新款晶
片推出，故反而仍能向上。目前展望 4Q26 亦維持正向看法，營收變化主要仍需視
後段封測產能的供給程度而定。研究部預估矽力杰 3Q26 毛利率 47.63%，稅後獲
利 11.32 億元(-28%QoQ，+66%YoY)，稅後 EPS 2.9 元，並預估 4Q26 營收 76.9
億元(+5.5%QoQ，+43%YoY)，毛利率 47.8%，稅後獲利 12.33 億元(+9%QoQ，
+53%YoY)，稅後 EPS 3.13 元。 
主要產能瓶頸在後段封測 ：目前矽力杰最主要的產能瓶頸在後段封測的支應，主要
是後段封測供給被 AI 應用排擠，目前除原有封測廠的產能擴充外，公司策略也持
續驗證新封測廠，以其紓解封測產能緊缺問題。至於晶圓產能，則矽力杰因具備第
四代 12 吋製程平台產能(第四代製程平台滲透率由 2025 年的個位數比重 ， 已將提
升至 4Q26 的 20%)，故反而晶圓較能充足的支應，明年第五代製程平台亦將加入
量產範圍。針對 2026 年毛利率走勢仍較有變數，係因影響毛利率因子正負交錯：
正向因子包括公司依據產品與客戶別而有不等的漲價措施 、 第四代製程平台挹注與
產品結構變化，負向因子則包括晶圓/封測成本上升、存貨依據庫齡政策而有損失
或回沖認列等影響。不過研究部認為隨 07/01 已開始進行漲價，理應能消弭部分
製造成本攀升的衝擊，遂預估 2H26 毛利率應能優於 2Q26 水準，中長期隨第四
代製程平台滲透率提升與產品結構變化， 理應有助於毛利率趨勢向上。 相較毛利率
是否獲得顯著提升，目前公司更關心在希冀產能能獲得更充足的支援， 以滿足客戶
的需求。 
資料中心與車載為後續成長主軸：資料中心、車載與工業將成為推升後續成長的主
軸：(1) 資料中心(包括主板/企業級 SSD/光模組)目前營收比重 15%~16%，2027
年 目 前 維 持 預 期>20% 比 重 ， 公 司資 料 中 心 三 大 項 目 比 重 依 序 為光 通 訊
>SSD>MB，其中光通訊與 SSD 個別均為 mid-to-high single digit，MB 比重較
低。公司過往以 400G 以下為主，今年 400G/800G 均已導入，隨市占率逐步提
升，又未來若主板核心電源出貨，更將進一步推動資料中心業務規模擴大。公司亦
表示 PSU 相關產品出貨亦隨伺服器需求而增，公司 PSU 產品包括 AC-DC 
controller/ESD/Boost/ Buck/Temp sensor等，目前針對 VPD 進展還沒那麼快。
(2) 車載營收比重可望由去年的 13%， 攀升至今年的15%~20%， 研究部認為車載
動力來自於公司車載晶片供應品項增加， 每台車的可供應的內含價值擴大， 並且客
戶群攀升，除中國品牌上量外，也進一步拓展海外市場。(3) 儲能與光伏需求，以
及充電樁應用，亦能拓展工業領域的需求。 
維持買進： 研究部預估矽力杰 2026 年營收 258.35 億元(+37%YoY)，毛利率
47.65%，稅後獲利 47.05 億元(+90%YoY)，稅後 EPS 11.95 元(原預估 11.59 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 341.9 億元(+32%YoY)，毛利率 48.44%，稅後獲利 60.39 億
元(+28%YoY)，稅後 EPS 15.34 元。考量矽力杰資料中心與車載仍將持續帶動營

營運現況與分析

運拓展，研究部維持買進建議，目標價維持 644 元(42X 2027 年 EPS 評價)。

1 
8422 可寧衛(Cleanaway)

報告日期： 2026/10/22 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 高雄 205 兵工廠專案整治案，己於 2024~2025 年分別認列營
收金額 2 億元與 12 億元，初期主要以開挖為主，掩埋次之，
預期進入中後期掩埋高峰階段，預估 2026~2027 年營收金額
10 億元與 10 億元。  
 小港焚化新爐興建，在 BOT 會計制度，總興建成本 350 億元，
工程期間 3.5 年，在興建期間需認列工程成本當作營收認列，
毛利率為零 ， 宏遠推估新爐2026 年貢獻營收金額 25 億元 ，2027
年貢獻營收金額 40 億元。  
一. 預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 0.33 元 
在 BOT 興建工程認列金額較 2Q26 減少因素，可寧衛 3Q26 營收
金額 19.89 億元 ，YoY+88.45% ，QoQ-12.29% ， 預估營業利益5.28
億元， YoY+44.72% ，QoQ-4.47% ，預估稅後盈餘 4.46 億元，
YoY+58.37% ，QoQ-2.79% ，預估稅後 EPS 0.33 元。  
二. 預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 0.37 元 
在 4Q26 興建工程進入高峰，預估可寧衛 4Q26 營收金額 22.09
億元， YoY+122.16% ，QoQ+11.04% ，預估營業利益 6.23 億元，
YoY+78.56% ， QoQ+17.95% ，預估稅後盈餘 5.09 億元，
YoY+82.15% ，QoQ+14.12% ，預估稅後 EPS 0.37 元。  
三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 1.25 元 
可寧衛 2026 年雖然在 205 兵工廠整治工程認列金額減少 ， 但2026
年 205 兵工廠營收認列主要以高毛利掩埋比重為主，帶動整體獲
利呈現小幅成長。預估可寧衛 2026 年營收金額 75.80 億元，
YoY+58.99% ，預估營業利益 20.87 億元， YoY+7.54% ，預估稅
後盈餘 16.95 億元， YoY+16.2% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.25 元。  
四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 1.35 元 
可寧衛 2027 年在 205 兵工廠掩埋處理量小幅增加，加上大承再
生粒料廠貢獻全年度營運，預期可寧衛 2027 年營運將持續成長。
宏遠預估可寧衛 2027 年營收金額 96.00 億元， YoY+26.65% ，預
估營業利益 22.97 億元 ，YoY+10.08% ， 預估稅後盈餘18.35 億元 ，
YoY+8.24% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.35 元。  
五. 投資建議買進，目標價 34 元 
觀察可寧衛歷史 PER11~30X ，2026 年 ROE 16.9% ，PER 21X ，
2027 年 ROE17% ，PER 19.3X 。評價偏低，故投資建議買進，目
標價 34 元（ 25X2027 年稅後 EPS ）。

2

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
可寧衛成立於 1999 年，經營有害事業廢棄物之處理業務，資本額
13.59 億元，主要業務包含有害重金屬污泥廢棄物固化清理、鋼鐵
業集塵灰固化清理、都市垃圾焚化爐飛灰固化清理、有害石綿廢
棄物固化清理與非法棄置場址整治業務，可寧衛為全國最大一站
式開挖、清運、固化、掩埋垂直整合之合法環保公司。可寧衛旗
下有五間子公司，其中岡聯事業以廢棄物清運為主、大倉實業以
一般事業廢棄物掩埋場、可寧衛企業以固化物專屬掩埋場、吉衛
以固化物專屬掩埋場、可寧衛投資主要負責中國大陸業務為主。

圖一、可寧衛集團

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

二. 現行廢棄物處理方式與市場概況  
廢棄物主要分成一般廢棄物與事業廢棄物，一般廢棄物主要以家
庭垃圾為主，在經過分類回收後，將進行焚化再中間處理，其中
可寧衛為最終掩埋廠。事業廢棄物在經過資源回收，再區分成有
害廢棄物與一般事業廢棄物，經過焚化處理後，再進行中間處理
（固化處理），最終進入掩埋。可寧衛為最終的固化處理與掩埋。

三.可寧衛公司經營模式與客戶概況  
可寧衛主要客戶分兩大類，第一類為製程客戶，如鋼鐵廠、焚化
爐、電子公司、汙水處理廠等，生產過程中排放出來的廢棄物，
如汞、鋅、銅或其他金屬，首先交由岡聯事業清運，分出一般廢
棄物與有害廢棄，如果是一般廢棄物由大倉事業負責掩埋，若是
有害廢棄物則先做固化處理 ， 再交由可寧衛企業/吉衛來負責掩埋 。

Company Report 
公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

4

第二類客戶為汙染清理客戶，主要處理非法棄置場址與關廠及土
水法列場址，可寧衛歷年來在關廠廠址業務有新竹飛利浦竹北
廠、桃園日商太平洋玻璃廠、台塑前鎮廠、南亞林園 DOP 廠、中
石化前鎮廠、台電興邦獅甲段、台肥前鎮廠、高雄榮民工程等。

圖四、可寧衛服務流程

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

四. 2026 年營運概況及 2027 年展望  
營收來源主要分成製程客戶與汙染清理客戶。在製程客戶營收淡
旺季主要需視客戶淡旺季而定， 1Q 在工作天數與下游客戶出貨量
較少情況下，為傳統淡季，之後逐季走高至 3Q，而後 4Q 將會略
為下滑。在汙染清理客戶方面，主要視案量大小與時間長短而定，
無明顯淡旺季之分。

可寧衛營收結構包括廢棄物固化及開挖、廢棄物掩埋、廢棄物清
運服務、再生紙銷售、預拌混凝土銷售、太陽能發電與其他共七
項。可寧衛 2025 年營收概況，廢棄物固化及開挖 2025 年營收金
額 17.71 億元， YoY-18.7% ；廢棄物掩埋 2025 年營收金額 15.8
億元 ，YoY-10.0% ； 廢棄物清運服務2025 年營收金額 1.44 億元，
YoY+37.1% ；再生紙銷售 2025 年營收金額 4.30 億元，
YoY-35.0% ；預拌混凝土銷售 2025 年營收金額 2.39 億元，
YoY+131.6% ；太陽能發電 2025 年營收金額 5.75 億元，
YoY+63.1% 。

廢棄物固化及開挖 2025 年營收金額 17.71 億元， YoY-18.7% 。觀
察營收衰退主因在於高雄中油煉油廠專案整治工程帶動
2023~2024 年廢棄物固化及開挖營收維持在相對高檔水準。 2025
年僅有 205 兵工廠專案，且開挖與固化比重相對較少因素。預期
2026 年，在 205 兵工廠專案整治工程減少，加上國內石綿瓦專案
固化金額未能彌補 205 兵工廠專案 ， 預估廢棄物固化及開挖2026
年營收金額 8.20 億元， YoY-53.7% 。預期 2027 年，在石綿瓦專
案逐漸結束，固化處理量減少，預估廢棄物固化及開挖 2027 年營
收金額 7.50 億元， YoY-8.5% 。

廢棄物掩埋服務 2025 年營收金額 15.8 億元， YoY-10.0% ，觀察
衰退主因在於 2025 年在 205 兵工廠專案，掩埋量相較高雄中油
專案來的低。預期 2026 年，在製程客戶掩埋量小幅增加，但在
205 兵工廠專案掩埋金額小幅增加 ， 預估廢棄物掩埋2026 年營收

億元， YoY+1.90% 。

再生紙銷售主要以上評資源為主。上評資源主要產品為各類牛皮
紙，瓦楞芯紙，工業管用紙，特殊用紙及植生資材之製造及分條
加工。再生紙銷售 2025 年營收金額 4.3 億元， YoY-35.0% 。預估
再生紙銷售 2026 年營收金額 1.7 億元， YoY-60.5% 。預計 4Q26
結束再生紙業務， 2027 年無營收貢獻。

預拌混凝土銷售主要以吉鼎材料為主。吉鼎 CLSM 廠收受再生粒
料，經過混凝土預拌製程，製程控制性低強度回填材料 (CLSM) ，
使用於道路基底層，低密度再生透水混凝土等，將廢棄物產品化，
拓展廢棄物一貫處理流程。吉鼎己於 2Q~3Q24 正式商轉，貢獻
2024 年營收金額 1.03 億元。 2025 年貢獻全年度營運，預拌混凝
土銷售 2025 年營收金額 2.39 億元，YoY+131.6% 。預期 2026 年
第二條產線配合大承再生粒料 4Q26 正式投產，預估預拌混凝土
銷售 2026 年營收金額 3.2 億元， YoY+34.1% 。預期 2027 年在搭
配大承開始投產，帶動預拌混凝土出貨量持續增加，預估預拌混
凝土 2027 年營收金額 4.2 億元， YoY+31.3% 。

太陽能發電主要以大創綠能為主 ， 大創綠能為可寧衛持股 100% 孫
公司。大創綠能主要位於高雄彌陀區設立綠電共生廠，將分三期
規劃，其中第一期裝置容量 69MW ，第二三期裝置容量
53~60MW 。第一期將投入 40 億元建置， 05/2024 開始營運，貢
獻 2024 年營收金額 3.52 億元。在 2025 年貢獻全年度營運，太
陽能發電 2025 年營收金額 5.75 億元， YoY+63.1% 。預期 2026
年，在發電量小幅增加，預估 2026 年太陽能發電營收金額 7.20
億元， YoY+25.3% 。預估 2027 年，在平均單價小幅增加，太陽
能發電營收金額 7.50 億元， YoY+4.20% 。

圖五、循環經濟—大承循環&吉鼎材料

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

### Page 7

Company Report 
公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

7

在其他營收方面， 2026 年將開始貢獻營收，包括高雄 AI 智能高
效焚化爐 BOT 案、大承再生粒料廠。

可寧衛 06/03/2025 公告取得 「高雄市AI 智能高效焚化爐 BOT 案
(小港 )」之最優申請人，可寧衛與台灣人壽成立 SPV 公司，其中
可寧衛持股 55%， 台灣人壽持股45%。 高雄小港焚化爐 ，BOT 案，
舊爐年處理量 34 萬噸，興建新爐年處理量 42 萬噸，營收來源包
括新焚化爐售電收入，新焚化爐代操作與舊焚化爐代操作，其中
新爐將於 2025 年下半年開始興建， 2028~2029 年正式營運，新
爐營運期 20 年。原有焚化爐將有代操作營運，己於 4Q25 正式移
交， 1Q26 開始小幅運作， 2Q26 正式營運，宏遠推估舊爐 2026
年處理量 22~26 萬噸，每噸操作收入 1700~2000 元，宏遠預估小
港焚化爐代操作貢獻 2026 年營收金額 2.5~3 億元（可寧衛持股
55% 進合併營收）。貢獻 2027 年營收金額 3~3.5 億元。

新爐興建方面，在 BOT 會計制度，總興建成本 350 億元，工程期
間 3.5 年，在興建期間需認列工程成本當作營收認列，宏遠推估
新爐 2026 年貢獻營收金額 25 億元， 2027 年貢獻營收金額 40 億
元，上述營收之毛利率為零。

大承再生粒料廠，可寧衛 100% 持股孫公司。大承再生粒料廠主要
使用固化及乾燥製程處理無機污泥 /灰渣 &有機污泥 /無害油泥等製
程再生粒料及資源化燃料，可收受代碼共 35 種（ D 類， R 類， S
類），月處理量 9000 噸，4Q26 末正式營運，預估 2026 年處理量
4.5~6 萬噸，每噸處理費用 6000~8000 元，宏遠預估大承再生粒
料貢獻 2026 年營收金額 0.5~1 億元 。 預估大承再生粒料貢獻2027
年營收金額 5 億元。

圖六、可寧衛能源

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

### Page 8

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

8

可寧衛能源，可寧衛持股 75% 。可寧衛能源主要將在桃園觀音地
區設立固體再生燃料  (SRF) 發電廠，投入 30~35 億元興建， 2022
年底動工。 SRF 發電廠是以回收塑料與廢紙、木材等生質物轉製
成的燃料來發電，再生燃料除了碳排放量大幅低於煤炭外，也能
作為工業鍋爐燃料使用，用途範圍廣泛。可寧衛能源主要營收來
源有兩部分，處理費與售電收入。

可寧衛能源回收塑料與廢紙等生質物，日處理量 350 噸，推估年
處理量 10 萬噸，每噸處理費用 7000 台幣，推估每年營收金額 7
億元。售電收入：再生燃料發電廠裝置容量 17MW ，全年發電度
數 1.2 億度，扣除自用 15% ，每度電 4.74 元計算，年營收金額
4.8 億元 。 宏遠預估2Q~3Q27 正式營運 ， 保守原則先不列入2027
年營收貢獻中。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

預估 2026 年稅後盈餘 16.95 億元， YoY+16.2% ，稅後 EPS 1.25
元

可寧衛 2026 年雖然在 205 兵工廠整治工程認列金額減少 ， 但2026
年 205 兵工廠營收認列主要以高毛利掩埋比重為主，帶動整體獲
利呈現小幅成長。宏遠預估可寧衛 2026 年營收金額 75.80 億元，
YoY+58.99% ，預估營業利益 20.87 億元， YoY+7.54% ，預估稅
後盈餘 16.95 億元， YoY+16.2% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.25 元。 (以 1
股分割 10 股後股數計算 )

預估 2027 年稅後盈餘 18.35 億元， YoY+8.24% ，稅後 EPS 1.35
元

可寧衛 2027 年在 205 兵工廠掩埋處理量小幅增加，加上大承再
生粒料廠貢獻全年度營運，預期可寧衛 2027 年營運將持續成長。
宏遠預估可寧衛 2027 年營收金額 96.00 億元， YoY+26.65% ，預
估營業利益 22.97 億元 ，YoY+10.08% ， 預估稅後盈餘18.35 億元 ，
YoY+8.24% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.35 元。 (以 1 股分割 10 股後股數
計算 )

주가 추이

3Q 큰 폭의 판매 감소, 매출/영업이익 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영 업 이 익 은 각각 44.6조원(-4.5% 이하 Y oY)과 2.4조원
(-5.4%) 기록하며 시장 기대치 하회 전망. 3Q 매출액은 글로벌 판매 부진 지속
과 환율 하락, 그리고 친환경차 판매 성장 둔화 등의 영향에 기인. 특히, 3Q 판
매량은 918.7만대(-11.5% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동기 대비 큰 폭 감소하며 매출
액에 부정적 영향. 이는 7월과 8월 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생
산 물량 차질이 발 생 한 부분에 기인. 파업은 기존 물량/믹스 개선에 기 여 했 던 
글로벌 HEV 판매도 둔화(+3.4%)시키면서 매출에 부정적 영향을 심화. 
3Q 수 익 성 은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입에 따른 원가 상승 영향과 친환경차 
판매 감소에 따른 부정적 믹스 영향 불 가 피. 이에 시장 기대치인 3.0조원을 크
게 하회할 전망. 다만, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮아지면서 판매보증비 
외화환산손익 영향은 다소 개선(0.4%p) 예상되며, 관세 영향 또한 전년 동기 
대비 약 1조원(2.1%p) 감소하면서 수익성 악화 영향을 일부 만 회 할 전망.  
’27년 지연된 신차효과 가시화로 반등 여력 충분 
4Q부터는 3Q 파업 영향 등으로 지연된 신차 확대 본격화되고, 현지 수요 높은 
차종 중심의 물량 확대를 통해 매출 회복 및 이익 성장 가시화가 기대. 아 반 떼
와 투싼 FMC 확대로 국내 및 해외 판매 둔화가 점진적 만회되고, 지난 9월부
터 터키에서 아이오닉3 생산이 본격화되면서 유럽에서의 시장 부진 회복이 기
대. 또한, GV80과 G80 HEV 모델 출시로 내년부터 국내/북미 중심 판매 확대
되며 올해 부진했던 물량/믹스 개선에 기여할 것으로 기대. ’27년 HMGMA에
서 HEV 신차 생산 투입 예정되어 있어 상 대 적 으 로 높은 수요가 지속되는 북미
에서의 HEV 현지 생산 확대로 가동률 증 가 와 관세 부담 완화도 수익성 개선에 
기여할 것으로 기대. 
부정적 환율 고려 필요하나 ’27년 강한 증익 기대  
’27년 동사 영 업 이 익 추 정 치 는 12.4조원(+22.3%)으로 ’26년 감 익 에 서 ’27년 
증익 전환 예상. 최근 환율 추세 고려 시 내년 상 반 기 까 지 부정적인 환율 영향 
확대 가 능 성 존재하나, 기존 노후 차 종 에 서 현지 수요 대응 가능한 신차 중심 
생산/판매 확대로 물량/믹스 개선되며 부 정 적 환율 영향 상쇄 기대.  
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-11.8%)과 상반기 적 용 한 코 스 피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)에 따라 목 표 주 가 를 670,000원으로 하향. 그러나, 연 내 RMAC 운영 
성 과 와 RA 설립, 12 월 모셔널 자 율 주 행 상용화와 웨 이 모向 로 보 택 시 
양산/공급 등 미래 사 업 에 서 의 가 시 성 도 여전히 기대 가능한 부분.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계 
금융투자상품의 비중 96.4% 3.6% 0.0% 100.0%

Document 279: BoJ preview_ Hawkish hold

Source PDF: all report pdf/BoJ preview_ Hawkish hold.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

After September’s policy rate hike to 1.25%, the Bank of Japan
is expected to deliver a hawkish hold tomorrow. In focus will be
the release of the October outlook report featuring the latest
forecasts for growth and inﬂation. CPI projections above 2% for
FY27 and FY28 should support expectations of rate hikes to
1.75-2.00% next year
Chris Turner
Bank of Japan headquarters in Tokyo
Price expectations well above 2%
Having hiked rates by 25bp to 1.25% in September, the central

messages from the BoJ were largely similar; upside price risks
remained, real interest rates were still negative and further policy
rate hikes should be expected.
Perhaps the only reason that a rate hike is not expected at this 30
October meeting is that the BoJ has never delivered back-to-back
rate hikes since it was given monetary policy independence in
1998. A hike in October could therefore smack of urgency.

### Page 2

Behind a widely expected unchanged decision will be a focus on the
voting pattern. Did arch-hawk Hajime Takata again dissent in
favour of back-to-back hikes? His vote for a hike would not be a
surprise. Anyone joining him would.
More interest will be had in the October outlook report. The July
report was pretty hawkish in that the median forecast for core (all
items less food and energy) inﬂation for FY27 and FY28 were 2.6%
and 2.2%, respectively. Let’s see how they change in the new

release.
Perhaps one comfort for the BoJ is that inﬂation expectations do
not seem to have worsened. The September Tankan report showed
business expectations for inﬂation fractionally dipping from the
highs, with PPI also showing early signs of topping out.
BoJ Tankan price expectations stabilise
Source: Bank of Japan
Market reaction
Markets currently price in an 80% chance of a 25b rate hike in
December and then another 50bp by next September. While

government representatives may well want the BoJ to go slow with
its tightening, the case against the BoJ taking rates up to the 1.75-
2.00% area is a weak one. Here the BoJ remains concerned with a
weak yen, broadening AI demand and elevated energy prices.

### Page 3

There does not seem an obvious case for a big move in market
rates or the yen on the back of Friday’s BoJ decision. A hawkish Fed
keeping USD/JPY bid in a broad 157-159 range into year-end is our

current view.
One low-probability, high-impact risk for Friday is any
announcement from Japan’s GPIF pension on re-allocating funds
domestically. We made the case in this article that a greater
allocation to domestic securities would help the government’s
huge investment drive. One of the most memorable adjustments in
those allocations was made on the same day as the BoJ’s policy
decision in October 2014.
Reports since the summer have tended to play down the prospects

of an allocation shift, and market attention has drifted away from
this subject. Any surprise announcements here could trigger a sub-
155 move in USD/JPY.

TUẦN 12-16/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
VN-Index có thể hồi kỹ thuật từ cạnh dưới tam giác, song việc chớm thủng hỗ trợ 
1.710 điểm và số mã còn giữ xu hướng tăng từ đáy tháng 7 suy gi ảm khiến xu 
hướng ngắn hạn trở nên trung tính.

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

### Page 4

4

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu 
Mã Giá đóng cửa tuần Biến động từ đầu tháng Giá khuyến nghị mua Giá mục tiêu Lợi nhuận  kỳ vọng Điểm nhấn đầu tư Báo cáo phân tích 
FRT 141.00 -4.1% 143.40 165.00 15.1% 
- Sau khi phủ sóng các thành phố lớn, chuỗi Long Châu đang mở rộng sang các vùng cấp 2, 3. 
Năm 2026 dự kiến mở thêm 400-450 cửa hàng (+20%YoY) với DT trên mỗi cửa hàng cũ tăng 
trưởng 6%-8%/năm 
- Dư địa lấy thêm thị phần từ các cửa hàng nhỏ lẻ vẫn còn tương đối rộng, FRT đặt mục tiêu 
chiếm 50% thị phần doanh thu ngành bán lẻ dược phẩm, từ mức 17%-20% hiện tại 
- Tiềm năng dài hạn từ mảng tiêm chủng tương đối tích cực

Economics | 3

### Page 4

Monthly Economics
Sector Analysis
Personal Consumption Expenditures (PCE)
• We've lifted our Q3 expectation for real PCE on incoming data and a slightly firmer consumer
backdrop in the wake of recent revisions.
Consumer spending looks firmer in the third quarter, and we now expect real PCE to rise at a 3.4%
annualized clip. This upward revision reflects recent data, with August consumption coming in firm
with real spending rising by the fastest monthly clip in nearly two years, but also reflects a slightly

firmer consumer backdrop in the wake of the BEA's annual revisions released late September. Although
revisions did not materially alter spending, they lifted the level of household income in recent years,
which also boosted the personal saving rate. The trajectory of real income growth remains weak, but
the consumer still looks to be in a slightly stronger financial position than previously believed, which
helps explain recent resilience in spending and suggests consumers may have a bit more runway ahead.

Investment: Equipment, Intellectual Property Products and Inventories
• The AI build out will lead to another surge in business fixed investment spending in Q3, though the
effect on headline growth will be partly offset by stronger imports.
Business fixed investment spending continues to be supported primarily by the high-tech buildout.
We forecast real equipment investment to rise at around a 16% annualized clip in Q3, but part of
this boost to headline GDP growth will be offset by stronger import growth that is facilitating the

build out. Net imports of computers are tracking to surge in the third quarter, which will provide a
sizable lift to information processing equipment investment, or around half of overall equipment.
And while non-defense capital goods shipments (including aircraft) slipped in August, the near-term
growth trajectory remains supportive of a pickup in equipment. The rebuild in inventories we have
been anticipating is also tracking to be a bit stronger as businesses replenish drawn down product.

Investment: Residential
• The abrupt rise in mortgage rates is likely to dampen residential investment over the next few
quarters. Residential spending should find support next year as longer-term interest rates
moderate somewhat.
We are unlikely to see another positive residential investment reading in Q3. Rising mortgage rates

have sent a chill over builder outlooks, pushing sales expectations to their weakest level in more than
three years. While builders have responded with deeper price cuts, affordability pressures continue
to weigh on single-family sales and construction outlays. Apartment construction is simultaneously
soft amid rising financing costs and modest rent growth, and home improvement spending remains
constrained by high materials costs and interest rates. While residential investment is likely to remain

4 | Economics

### Page 5

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
a drag this year, we expect a slight moderation in longer-term interest rates in 2027, which would
provide a more supportive backdrop.
Investment: Nonresidential Structures
• We have not made material changes to our nonresidential structures forecast. Despite elevated
interest rates, data center and related spending should continue to support structures investment
through our forecast horizon.
Nonresidential structures spending has accelerated sharply, overwhelmingly driven by data centers

and electric power facilities. As data center construction expands geographically and demand rises
for semiconductors, high-tech capex has also started to generate positive spillovers for warehousing
and manufacturing construction. This buildout, which appears relatively insensitive to interest rates,
should be enough to sustain solid growth in structures investment even as spending across most
other categories remains essentially flat. Domestic energy is another green shoot, with elevated prices

supporting ongoing growth in active rotary rigs.

Net Exports & Trade
• An expected drag from trade in Q3 got larger in light of a near-record trade deficit in August, net
exports is now expected to lop off three full percentage points from third quarter growth.

Exports increased in August for the first time in four months, but the increase was more than offset by
an even larger increase in imports. The upshot was a widening in the trade deficit to a near-record gap
of more than $105 billion. Going into the report, we had a 1.9 percentage point drag from net exports
penciled in for Q3 GDP; we revised that to a full 3.0 percentage points drag in light of the record gap.
Even a modest narrowing in the final months of the year would likely mean a lift to headline GDP in

Q4, but the real story here is that trade will remain a headwind throughout the forecast period owing
largely to surging imports of tech-related products amid the AI boom.
Labor Market
• The jobs market remains stable. We continue to expect the unemployment rate to end the year at
4.2% and hold near that level in 2027.
We modestly revised down our near-term payroll growth forecast following the September jobs
report, but otherwise we have left our labor market outlook unchanged. Measures of labor demand

growth continue to point to a stable labor market. Job openings have inched higher on a year-ago
basis but remain broadly steady while initial jobless claims, WARN notices and Challenger job cut
announcements continue to signal low layoffs. Labor force participation appears to be improving after
its sharp pullback in the first half of the year. Looking ahead, we expect payroll growth to average
around 60K per month in 2027, roughly in line with our estimate of breakeven job growth. That should

Economics | 5

### Page 6

Monthly Economics
keep the unemployment rate near 4.2% in 2027 and leave labor market conditions broadly consistent
with "full employment." Notably, our expectation for labor cost growth of ~3.4% is the reason we
continue to reiterate that labor cost growth is not a contributor to the excess inflation seen in the
data.
Inflation
• We expect the core PCE deflator to rise 3.2% and 2.6% Q4/Q4 in 2026 and 2027, respectively.
Headline inflation remains heavily influenced by developments in the Middle East, with higher oil

prices lifting gasoline and diesel prices. Food inflation has been tame this year but looks poised to
strengthen as higher transportation and commodity prices work their way through supply chains.
Beyond food and energy, price pressures remain concentrated in a handful of categories, most notably
airfares and AI-related tech goods. Core goods inflation is showing signs of stabilizing, as waning tariff-
related price pressures have been offset by resilient demand tied to the AI buildout. We expect some

improvement here in 2027. Still-low shelter inflation and cooler price growth for airfares and health
care should help bring about some moderation in core services inflation next year. We expect the core
PCE deflator to rise 3.2% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis in 2026 before slowing to 2.6% in 2027.

Fiscal Policy
• Fiscal year (FY) 2026 ended on September 30, and the federal government tallied a $2.0 trillion
budget deficit (6.5% of GDP). We project the deficit to be unchanged in dollar terms in FY 2027

and a bit smaller as a share of GDP (~6%).
• Another continuing resolution to extend government funding past the December 11 deadline
seems like the most likely outcome at this time for the FY 2027 budget. It is possible Republicans
use the lame duck session of Congress to increase defense spending and raise the debt ceiling.
Looking ahead to the lame duck session of Congress that will follow the midterm elections, Congress
needs to pass a bill that extends government funding past December 11 to avert a government

shutdown. Another continuing resolution that punts the issue into Q1 seems like the most likely
outcome. Perhaps more interesting will be whether Republicans attempt to use budget reconciliation
to pass one more partisan piece of legislation. If they do, we suspect the two things at the top the
agenda will be more funding for national defense and immigration enforcement priorities as well as a
debt ceiling increase. For the latter, raising it in the lame duck would remove some leverage Democrats

could have in 2027 if they recapture one or both chambers. It is still very early, but we estimate the
debt ceiling "X date" by which Congress must act or face default is sometime in late Q3/early Q4 of
next year.

### Page 7

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
Monetary Policy & Interest Rates
• Our base case forecast is for the FOMC to hike the fed funds rate by 25 bps in December and
then keep the policy rate unchanged in 2027 at 4.00%-4.25%, with risks skewed toward additional
tightening.
As we go to print, the FOMC seems more likely than not to keep the fed funds rate unchanged in
October. But given the rationale under which the Fed started this tightening cycle and the forcefulness

with which Warsh has driven home his displeasure with elevated inflation, it seems entirely plausible
to us that October will be a live meeting. At a minimum, the recent inflation data seem to make a hike
in December a lock. Past December, the outlook will be highly dependent on future inflation data. Our
expectation is that core inflation will cool gradually in 2027, and we still do not see signs of demand-
side inflation in the data. But hiking cycles have an inertial component to them, and the recent inflation

data reset the clock to zero on the Fed seeing several good inflation prints in a row. This creates upside
risk to our base case for the fed funds rate.

International Developments
• Global growth remains resilient, leaving our 2026 and 2027 forecasts unchanged. Our inflation
forecasts are also unchanged, although risks of delayed price pass-through could prolong inflation
pressures into 2027.

• Monetary policy continues to lean toward tightening across advanced economies, while emerging
market central banks are increasingly cautious, either holding rates or easing more gradually as
inflation pressures rebuild.
The balance of risks to global inflation remains tilted to the upside, complicating the monetary policy
outlook even as growth has proven more resilient than expected. Elevated energy prices, particularly
for refined petroleum products, persistent geopolitical uncertainty and El Niño-related food price

risks could prolong inflationary pressures into 2027. While second-round effects still remain relatively
contained, repeated supply shocks are testing central banks’ willingness to look through the temporary
price increases, particularly where inflation expectations remain elevated, even as higher government
bond yields are already contributing to tighter financial conditions.
Across advanced economies, central banks face increasingly difficult trade-offs as persistent inflation

pressures limit the scope for policy accommodation. The Eurozone remains particularly vulnerable
to energy supply disruptions, with elevated natural gas prices and low storage levels adding to
inflation risks. We continue to expect a 25 bps European Central Bank hike in December, followed by a
prolonged hold through 2027. Fiscal concerns are also adding to pressure, particularly in France, where
the selloff in government debt has pushed yields higher. In the U.K., resilient activity and renewed

Economics | 7

### Page 8

Monthly Economics
inflation pressures similarly underpin our expectation for a Bank of England hike in November,
followed by a hold through 2027.
Elsewhere, policy paths are becoming more differentiated. After four hikes this year brought the Cash
Rate to 4.60%, we expect the Reserve Bank of Australia to remain on hold, although renewed inflation
pressures or stronger-than-expected activity could prompt further tightening. We also still expect
the Bank of Canada to hold at 2.25%, with subdued growth weighing against energy-driven inflation

risks, although incoming inflation data over the coming weeks will be critical in determining whether
persistent price pressures warrant tightening monetary policy perhaps as early as the December
meeting. Japan remains an exception to the broader hold outlook: while the yen's recovery provides
some relief from imported inflation, elevated energy prices and import dependence continue to pose
upside risks. We expect the Bank of Japan to hike again by year-end and now expect an additional 25

bps hike in Q3-2027, bringing the terminal rate to 1.75%.
The emerging market story has increasingly shifted from easing toward patience or selective
tightening. In Asia, China's weak domestic demand and persistent property-sector strains continue to
warrant policy support, with AI-related exports providing some offset. We expect a more gradual pace
of Reserve Requirement Ratio cuts alongside targeted monetary and fiscal measures. India faces the

opposite challenge, with elevated food and energy prices and rupee depreciation strengthening the
case for further Reserve Bank of India tightening. In Latin America, peso depreciation and elevated
energy costs pose upside inflation risks for Mexico, although limited exchange-rate pass-through
should allow Banxico to remain on hold while policymakers monitor underlying inflation and inflation
expectations. In Brazil, election-related uncertainty and renewed inflation pressures are likely to delay

further easing, with cuts expected to resume in Q1-2027 once policymakers see more convincing
evidence of disinflation.

exports and investment and an intact semiconductor

consumption.

upstream costs.

### Page 2

Taiwan: AI, energy and 4Q outlook October 13, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

Global backdrop

The global landscape remains complex, 
shaped by the interplay of AI, energy 
prices and tariffs, with significant 
implications for growth, inflation and 
financial conditions.

1) AI

The debate over the durability of the AI 
investment boom continues.  Tailwinds 
include technological advances, broader 
adoption, and s trategic and geopolitical 
considerations. Headwinds range from 
uncertainty over investment returns  and 
hyperscaler financing pressure to 
physical bottlenecks , safety concerns, 
and regulatory uncertainty.

Hyperscaler financing pressure has 
attracted increasing attention . In the 
early stages of the AI investment boom, 
hyperscaler capex was largely financed 
by internal cash flow. This is now 
changing, as free cash flow is expected 
to turn negative from 4Q, requiring major 
players to rely on debt issuance and 
other external financing increasingly.

At the same time, higher global interest 
rates are raising the cost of AI 
investment. The Fed resumed rate hikes 
at its September meeting, while global 
bond yields remain under upward 
pressure amid persistent inflation and 
elevated public and private borro wing 
needs. Our baseline assumes US rates 
will rise by 50bps this year and a further 
25bps in 2027 to 4.50%, with global 
long-term rates also remaining under 
pressure. This could increasingly weigh 
on the economics and pace of AI 
investment.

US-China AI competition is also drawing 
increasing attention.  Across the five 
layers of the AI ecosystem, China is 
making rapid progress in energy, 
infrastructure, AI models and 
applications, supported by its large scale, 
cost-effective domestic manufacturing 
supply chains and huge consumer 
market, which provides a wide range of 
real-world scenarios for AI adoption. The 
emergence of a more integrated and 
cost-competitive Chinese AI ecosystem 
could increasingly challe nge the US 
ecosystem, which currently operates at a 
relatively higher cost.

China, however, remains relatively weak 
in semiconductors.  Around half of 
China's domestic AI chip market still 
relies on foreign suppliers, including 
lower-end NVIDIA chips. This 
semiconductor bottleneck remains a key 
constraint on China's AI development. In 
our view, Taiwanese semiconductor 
companies' competiti ve moat therefore 
remains largely intact in the near term.

2) Energy

The US -Iran conflict remains at a 
standoff, keeping the energy -price 
outlook highly uncertain.  New 
developments are likely after the US 
midterm elections in November, with 
both further escalation and de -
escalation possible. Crude oil prices have 
remained elevated at around 
USD100/bbl amid a persistent 
geopolitical risk premium . In contrast, 
refined product prices have risen more 
sharply as refining disruptions and 
tighter product logistics have amplified 
the supply shock. Natural gas prices 
have also increased sharply, reflecting 
disruptions to LNG supply and limited 
alternative shipping routes.

Taiwan has been actively diversifying 
its energy import sources to cushion the 
impact. LNG imports from Qatar have 
fallen to zero since April, while imports 
from the US and other suppliers have 
surged to offset the shortfall, helping 
maintain stable domestic electricity 
supply.

### Page 5

Taiwan: AI, energy and 4Q outlook October 13, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

3) Tariffs

US tariff actions are likely to accelerate 
after the US midterm elections in 
November. Two major tariff measures 
remain in the pipeline. The Section 301 
forced-labor tariff was implemented in 
July, while final tariff action under the 
Section 301 overcapacity investigation, 
which covers 16 economies, remains 
pending. Meanwhile, Phase 1 of the 
Section 232 semiconductor tariffs was 
implemented in January, while Phase 2, 
covering a broader range of 
semiconductor-related products, 
remains pending.

Taiwan has secured a relatively 
favourable framework through its trade 
and investment agreements with the 
US. Taiwan signed an Agreement on 
Reciprocal Trade (ART) and an 
Investment MOU with the US earlier this 
year. The ART establishes a 15% US tariff 
rate on Taiwanese goods, while the MOU 
provides Taiwan with preferential 
treatment under Section 232, including 
for semiconductors. The ART has yet to 
enter into force, and it remains to be 
seen how the final Section 301 tariff 
burden will interact with the 15% ART 
framework. Meanwhile, major Taiwanese 
companies such as TSMC are expanding 
their US investments under the 
Investment MOU, which could support 
relatively favourable treatment for 
Taiwan when broader Section 232 
semiconductor tariffs are introduced.

Taiwan 4Q outlook

Taiwan has navigated the complex 
global environment relatively well so far 
this year, supported by robust AI exports 
and investment, diversified energy 
imports, and efforts to absorb higher 
upstream energy costs. Some 
normalisation is likely in 4Q26 –2027, as 
the AI -driven growth boost moderates 
and domestic energy prices face greater 
pressure to adjust.

1) Growth

GDP growth remains broadly on track 
with our expectations. High -frequency 
indicators suggest that GDP growth 
should remain around 12% yoy in 3Q, 
broadly in line with 12.9% in 2Q but below 
the 15.4% peak in 1Q. Growth is expected 
to moderate to a high -single-digit pace 
from 4Q onwards. We maintain our full -
year GDP growth forecasts at 11.6% for 
2026 and 5.6% for 2027.

The AI cycle remains the key driver of 
Taiwan's growth.  Information and 
communication products and electronic 
components accounted for nearly 80% 
of total exports in the first nine months 
of this year, while manufacturing 
accounts for nearly 50% of gross fixed

capital formation. From 4Q onwards, 
export growth is expected to moderate 
from exceptionally strong levels to a 
more normal but still robust pace, 
broadly following the trajectory of 
global hyperscaler capex and 
semiconductor sales. Investment is likely 
to follow a similar path.

For now, Taiwanese technology 
companies continue to report robust 
revenue growth, led by semiconductors, 
ODMs/EMS, and PCs and servers. 
Capacity utilisation at leading Taiwanese 
foundries has also continued to rise, 
suggesting that investment demand 
remains strong.

The AI boom is also beginning to 
generate broader spillovers to the 
domestic economy. The AI-driven equity 
rally has generated significant wealth 
effects, with the TAIEX up 70% year-to-
date and Taiwan becoming the world's 
fifth-largest stock market. Taiwanese 
households have benefited from the 
appreciation of financial assets, with 
equities and mutual funds accounting for 
nearly 30% of total household financial 
assets. The equity rally has also 
generated positive income effects. Wage 
growth has picked up, led by the services 
sector, particularly financial services, as 
strong earnings at financial 
institutions—especially securities 
firms—have prompted higher 
compensation to retain talent. These 
wealth and income gains should support 
household consumption, creating a 
positive feedback loop that broadens the 
benefits of the AI boom across the wider 
economy.

2) Inflation

CPI inflation is also tracking broadly in 
line with our forecast. Headline CPI 
remained elevated at 2.4% yoy in 3Q, up 
from 1.2% in 1Q and 2.2% in 2Q. We 
maintain our forecast for headline CPI 
inflation to remain close to 2.5% in 4Q, 
and retain our full-year forecasts at 2.0% 
for 2026 and 2.1% for 2027.

Energy inflation remains the main 
upside risk.  The government has 
absorbed much of the increase in energy 
costs through state -owned CPC and 
Taipower, limiting the pass -through to 
domestic consumers. With losses at both 
companies widening, the government 
will need to decide whether to provide 
additional subsidies to cover the losses 
or allow further increases in domestic 
energy prices. The September re view 
postponed the adjustment to the 
December meeting, when the overall 
situation will be reassessed after the 
November local elections. Taipower's  
latest review indicated that electricity 
rates would need to rise by 12.8%. A hike 
in electricity prices could lift inflation 
expectations and generate second -
round effects, particularly as the labour 
market tightens and wage pressures 
increase.

3) Monetary policy

The central bank kept the policy 
discount rate unchanged at 2.00% at its 
September meeting, in line with our 
expectations. We continue to expect a 
modest 12.5bp rate hike at the 
December meeting, bringing the policy 
rate to 2.125%.  By then, CPI inflation is 
likely to have remained above 2% for 
around six consecutive months. The 
possibility of an increase in electricity 
prices following the November local 
elections could further lift inflation 
expectations, strengthening the case for 
a December rate hike.

Financial conditions have already 
tightened despite the absence of a 
policy rate hike.  M2 growth remained 
around 7% yoy in 3Q, well below double-
digit nominal GDP growth. At the same 
time, bank loan growth has accelerated 
to more than 10% yoy, driven by strong

corporate credit demand, particularly AI-
related fundraising by technology 
companies. Tighter financial conditions 
are also evident in rising market interest 
rates, including commercial paper rates 
and government bond yields.

The central bank has continued to ease 
housing credit controls gradually. At its 
September meeting, it raised the LTV 
cap on second -home mortgage loans to 
70%, the second easing of housing credit 
controls this year. There is scope for 
further easing, including removing the 
LTV cap on second-home loans or raising 
the LTV caps for third - and fourth-home 
loans and high -value properties, which 
currently stand at 30%. However, the 
central bank is likely to proceed 
cautiously, given concerns that a faster 
relaxation could reignite  speculative 
demand and put renewed upward 
pressure on housing prices.

4) Housing

A gradual and modest recovery in the 
housing market is expected from 4Q26 
into 2027.  The existing -home market 
has already shown early signs of 
recovery, with Sinyi  housing prices 
returning to slightly positive yoy growth 
since 2Q. Total building transactions also 
returned to positive yoy growth in 3Q. 
The easing of credit controls should help 
reinforce this modest recovery trend.

The new -home market remains under 
pressure. The number of new units under 
construction has started to decline since 
2023, while new completions are  only 
beginning to ease this year . Demand 
remains weak, as potential buyers 
continue to wait for lower prices. Unsold 
new homes stood at 113K units at end -
2025, compared with a historical normal 
level of around 80K, implying excess 
inventory of roughly 30K units. At the 
current pace of new -home transactions, 
it would take roughly one year to 
normalize the existing excess inventory, 
and longer when factoring in the new 
supply pipeline. Some industry experts 
estimate that it could take as long as 3.8 
years digest current inventories fully 
(here).

### Page 2

UNITED STATES 
The US economy is expected to grow above its potential in 2026, with an average annual growth rate of 2. 2%, close to the 2025 rate (2.1%) . 
This apparent resilience to the uncertainty, tariff and energy shocks, together with above-trend productivity growth, masks K-shaped growth, 
driven by investment linked to AI-optimism and consumption by the wealthiest amid a wealth effect (historically high stock market valuations). 
Inflation overshooting is set to continue (3.4% in 2026) at least through 2028, largely because of the rise in oil prices and tariffs – although the 
impact of these latter appears to be less significant than expected. The labour market is showing clear signs of improvement that should bring 
the unemployment rate down toward 4.0%. Given this shift in price and employment risks amid dynamic growth, the FOMC proceeded with a 
first rate hike at September’s meeting and we expect two further 25bp increases in December 2026 and January 2027, bringing the Fed Funds 
target range at 4,25% - 4,50%.

CHINA 
Economic growth slowed to 4.3% y/y in Q2 2026 vs. 5.0% in Q1. It is projected to reach 4.6% in 2026 as a whole, down from 5% in 2025. Growth 
remains characterized by a K-shaped trajectory, and the gap between the strong performance of exports and the fragility of sectors that rely 
on domestic demand  has even widened this year . Domestic demand has weakened significantly in recent months . Retail sales have barely 
increased, the crisis in the property sector has continued and household confidence remains low. Fiscal policy measures aimed at supporting 
economic growth are expected to increase in the second half of the year. CPI inflation has rebounded slightly since last March, mostly due to 
the rise in global energy prices, as well as due to the increase in electronic good prices and anti-involution measures implemented by the 
authorities. Deflationary pressures persist, however, given weakness in domestic demand.

EUROZONE 
Eurozone growth is expected to slow to 1.1% in 2026 (from 1.3% in 2025), before strengthening to 1.5% in 2027, supported by higher defence 
and infrastructure spending as well as the global AI capex cycle. The economy has proven resilient to the energy shock, notab ly thanks to the 
increasingly tangible impact of G erman defence spending on the real economy. Elevated energy prices in the autumn (especially for natural 
gas), combined with resilient growth, are set to accelerate inflation to a peak around the turn of the year. Annual average i nflation is thus 
expected to reach  3.0% in 2026 (from 2.1% in 2025), before easing to 2.8% in 2027. Given persistent inflation and resilient growth, we 
anticipate another 25bp hike from the ECB in December 2026, bringing the deposit rate to 2.75%, followed by an extended pause . That said, 
stronger than expected second-round effects could lead to more tightening than our current baseline.

### Page 3

FRANCE 
GDP growth remained low in Q2 (0% after -0.2% in Q1) as a result of deteriorated agricultural and construction output, as well as higher 
inflation (2.4% in 2026 according to our forecast, after 0.9% in 2025) . In 2026, GDP growth should  reach 0.5% (after 0.9% in 2025), driven 
mainly by exports.

UNITED KINGDOM 
UK GDP growth would ease slightly to 1.2% in 2026 (1.3% in 2025), before edging back to 1.3% in 2027. The economy has so far weathered 
both the energy price shock and domestic political volatility. That said, growth is expected to decelerate in the second half  of the year, while 
inflation is set to remain stubbornly above target (averaging 3.2% in both 2026 and 2027), driven by higher energy prices and mounting second-
round effects. In response, we expect the BoE to deliver a single insurance hike in November 2026, bringing the Bank Rate to 4.00%. The BoE 
is then expected to resume easing in H2 2027, with two cuts taking the Bank Rate to 3.50% by end-year.

JAPAN 
Following 1.2% in 2025, Japan's GDP growth is expected to decelerate to 1.0% in 2026, before rebounding to 1.2% in 2027. Higher inflation 
and production costs associated with the energy shock are expected to weigh on the economy’s overall performance. On the other hand, the 
latter is supported by fiscal policy  and AI-related investment. Inflation has generally overshot the 2% y/y target since 2022. Accordingly, the 
Bank of Japan initiated a cautious process of “adjustment in the degree of monetary accommodation” in 2024, lifting the policy rate to 1.25% 
so far (previously negative) – the highest since 1995. We expect two more hikes by the end of Q1 2027, followed by a return to a more cautious 
pace until the terminal rate of +2.5% is reached in H2 2028. Japan is facing long-term rates pressure, illustrated by historically high 10- and 30-
year yields, probably fueled by the level of public debt and the pace of monetary adjustment.

EXCHANGE RATES 
We expect Fed hikes to temporarily offset structural headwinds and support the dollar. EUR/USD would target 1.14 by end -2026, before the 
dollar's structural downtrend resumes toward 1.20 by end-2027, although political and fiscal risk may weigh on the euro next year. The USD/JPY 
appreciation would continue but at a more moderate pace, with the BoJ still behind the curve despite its hikes. By contrast, GBP/USD would 
see relative support underpinned by a modest fiscal risk premium under the new Labour government, and resilient growth.

1
《GLP-1公司展望分歧 ，製藥多受惠新藥銷售成長 》
近月GLP-1領域因市場轉關注諾和諾德 (NVO)在巴
西、加拿大等新興市場因 semaglutide專利到期 ，
在糖尿病症恐面臨降價壓力抵銷預期銷量倍數成長 ，
公司雖重申主要市場美國核心專利於 2032年才到期，

減重仍有其他專利保護 ，但計入五款重磅藥物 ，整
體2026–2030年營收複合年增長率(CAGR)僅與大型
製藥同業平均約4-7%年增率相似，使股價大幅下修。

《醫療器械喜憂參半，但必要性手術需求仍佳》
近月醫療服務設備股為健護中股價表現較差 ，主因：
1)亞培受到產品責任險達成部分和解協議 ，後續還
有其他聯邦 、州判決仍為股價壓力； 2)波士頓科學
受資安事件影響生產宣告無法達成財測 ，HRS的左

心耳封堵術 (LAAC) 指南適用 WATCHMAN、 亞培
Amulet，可同時做消融+LAAC，但LAAC不能完全
替代口服抗凝血藥物 (OAC)對預防中風的效用 ，仍
為二線，等同喜憂參半 。另受自付成本增加 ，預期
擇期、外科手術需求疲軟延伸至年底 ，機器人手術
成長率區間接近 14.5%，下半年低於上半年 ，將以
擴大翻新系統的布署降低門檻及增加門診手術需求 ，
擴大目標市場 ，但必要醫療手術需求健康 ，心血管
類手術下半年仍成長，醫療器材基本面喜憂參半。
《星級獎金與醫療成本控管力使長期獲利分化》
每月聯邦醫療保險優勢計畫投保人數成長趨勢持續 ，
CMS預計於2026年10月中公布 2027年醫療優勢計
畫(MA)星級評等，因評級變嚴將影響醫保公司會員
留存率、競爭力及2028年品質獎金收入不確定性增
加，低於4星級業者面臨品質獎金減少與長期獲利承
壓風險。另無意外法案獨立爭議解決 (IDR)仲裁成本
持續推升商業保險理賠支出 ，增加2027年雇主團體
及ACA市場保費定價壓力 。管理式醫療業者今年展

現利潤優先於市占率、砍不賺錢計畫/人數拉抬利潤
率，但明年若保費調漲幅度不及醫療成本增速 ，恐
推升醫療損失率 、壓縮承保利潤 。後續獲利關鍵在
於星級評等 、保費調整能力及理賠成本控管 ，業者
間的長期獲利表現可能進一步分化。

《近期資金轉向公用事業等防禦型類股 ，惟高利率
風險仍存》
聯準會10月FOMC會議維持目標利率在3.75~4%區
間，但隨著通膨仍在升溫 ，目前市場普遍預期聯準
會在今年12月及2027年上半年將再各升息一碼。公
用事業屬於資本密集型產業 ，極度依賴債務融資來
支持基礎設施建設與更新 ，升息提高了企業利息支
出、直接壓縮利潤空間 ，對公用事業獲利與產業評
價相對不利。
近期隨著部分 AI與經濟敏感型股票面臨估值修正與
財報疑慮，市場資金開始撤出高基期板塊 ，轉向醫
療保健與公用事業等相對保守 、現金流穩健的防禦
型領域，但在目前利率仍高的情況下 ，預估短期內

公用事業走勢將呈區間內來回震盪。

零售客流量表
重點摘要
• 美國多州出現暫停資料中心建
設的政策訴求 ，資金可能加速
流向規則較為明確的州。
• 電力股的投資邏輯正在轉向 ，
未來將更看重獲利品質與監管
可預測性。
• AI資料中心電力需求仍是公用
事業3Q26財報重點 ，但焦點轉
向如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成
本。
《部分電力股評價將更看重監管可預測性》
在2026年美國期中選舉涉及的36場州長選戰中，已
有20州出現暫停資料中心建設或許可的政策訴求 。
電價、用水與基礎設施成本的分攤爭議 ，正改寫各
州爭取AI投資的方向 ，資金可能從監管混沌的地區 ，
加速流向規則較為明確的州 。德州為這輪監管分化
中最值得關注 、也最棘手的地區 。德州電網接入申
請佇列已成長到約 474吉瓦，其中約九成來自資料

中心，而德州州長已宣布暫停核准新的資料中心接
入州內電網，9月底環境委員會的暫停令進一步擴及
自備電源的離網專案 ，意味企業即使自己發電 ，也
未必能繞過許可關卡。
相較之下，在大規模用電需求湧入前 ，先把監管制
度架好的州或許更具產業前景 。懷俄明、明尼蘇達、
愛荷華、威斯康辛 、內華達、南卡羅來納與田納西
州為潛在的資金承接者 。明尼蘇達已立法防止大客
戶把成本轉嫁給一般用戶 ，科羅拉多的大負荷費率
方案則要求至少15年合約、80%最低計費需求與提
前退出費 ，把部分退出風險交還給客戶 。電力股的
投資邏輯正在轉向 ，未來部分公用事業股的評價分
化，將從過去單純看需求規模 ，轉向更看重獲利品
質與監管可預測性。
《AI資料中心電力需求仍為近期財報重點 ，但焦點
轉向如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成本》
隨著3Q26財報陸續披露，美國公用事業公司對AI資
料中心用電需求普遍釋出極為樂觀且迫切的長線成
長展望。以FirstEnergy為例，受惠於毗鄰北弗吉尼
亞數據中心走廊的優勢 ，西弗吉尼亞州等地的資料

中 心 相 關 負 載 需 求 激 增 137% 達到 4.3 吉瓦 。
American Electric Power其累積的增量負載承諾已
飆升至56吉瓦，半年內暴增一倍 。但公用事業高層
的態度在積極擴產中也帶有審慎風險控管 ，為了避
免新建發電廠及電網升級的費用侵蝕一般用戶權益 ，
業者正重新設計合約 ，要求科技公司簽署最低電費
與信用擔保條款 。整體而言 ，公用事業財報反映出
資料中心用電需求並未降溫 ，但產業焦點已從 「如
何接到訂單 」轉向「如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成本 」
的階段。
